- **qa-expert**: `qa-expert` is an optional exploratory QA sidecar role. It is **not** part of the main deterministic SDLC phase sequence in [`../agent-workflow.md`](../agent-workflow.md). Use it when exploratory testing would add coverage beyond the deterministic `tester` phase. ([smota/agentflow-sdlc](https://github.com/smota/agentflow-sdlc/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/qa-expert.md))
- **qa-expert**: World-class QA engineer and testing expert. Use after code changes to write and update test cases, evals, and verify code quality. Thinks adversarially — finds the bugs developers miss. ([ssehrawat/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/ssehrawat/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-expert.md))
- **qa-explorer**: Use this agent to QA-test a single Medika frontend page like a real user trying to break it — running the negative/validation checks from docs/qa/check-catalog.md, capturing screenshots + console + network evidence, and returning STRUCTURED findings (it does not file issues; the /qa-sweep skill dedups and files). Invoke one per page. &lt;example&gt; Context: The orchestrator wants the profile page checked. user: "QA the /profile page on local." assistant: "I'll launch the qa-explorer agent against /profile and collect structured findings." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: A nightly sweep fans out one… ([akqira/Medika](https://github.com/akqira/Medika/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-explorer.md))
- **qa-explorer**: Read-only exploratory-QA agent for the protospy UI. Runs one charter at a time: injects a fixture state, drives the live app via playwright-cli as a real user would, and reports honest, confidence-rated findings — the contextual "while using it, something is off" bugs the unit/component/browser suites and the static visual review miss. Interaction-first, step-budgeted, and self-critiqued to suppress false positives. ([csw/protospy](https://github.com/csw/protospy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-explorer.md))
